Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any innate module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 508. The projective resolution has length at most 954 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the innate decomposition. By minimality, the element a^508 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 954-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
